Runir seu Danica literatura antiquissima (Ole Worm, 1636)
========================================
Worm's runology (this text from the 1651 second edition) — the first scholarly treatment of the runes; the founding work of runic studies. In Latin. Public domain. Source: Internet Archive (https://archive.org/details/bub_gb_Lf96CFoAOhQC).
